# 3.43 T3 — 2026-11-10: non-void definitions with no real `return` (HEAD 50da691580)

Method (.run/3.43/t3/scan.py, scratch; re-derive from this line): over `git ls-files src include` *.c/*.h, blank comments, strings and preprocessor lines; at brace depth 0 a `{` after a `type name(params)` head (K&R parameter declarations allowed) opens a definition; kept if the return type is not exactly `void` (`void *` counts) and the masked body has no `\breturn\b` (kind `noreturn`) or has a bare `return;` (kind `barereturn`). Last-statement bucket per copy, comments stripped: `call` (expression statement ending in a call, no `=`), `assign`, `block` (body ends in `}`: if/loop/switch), `bare` (`return;`), `other:<text>` (scanner could not split the tail; includes one-line defs).

Totals: 394 names, 9,009 definition sites (noreturn 358 names / 7,645 sites; barereturn 29 / 1,357; both kinds across copies 7). 67 names have 110-140 overlay copies (~8,600 sites); 311 single-def; 61 defined only in src/shared or include headers. `SHARED_FN` = the def name inside share_body cls_*.h headers (146 sites).
Cross-check: commit 112c3e4eb9 (3.41 T4.c2) flipped 38 names / 39 defs `void` -> non-void; all 38 are here (marked `flip`); 3.41 T4 reported 37. 3.43 T3 fixed the 38 (see phase-ends/phase-3.43/tasks/T3.md once archived); the other 356 are parked for Gen 4 (INBOX).

Format: `name defs=<n> shared=<n in headers> kinds=<k> last={bucket: copies} [flip]`.
